Front and back swap, and left and right swap.\nSo I find the step at my back-left.\n</think>\n\nB",

  "target": "<think>\nStep 1: find the air mattress, the headboard and the paper bag.\nStep 2: facing the headboard from the air mattress, I find the paper bag behind me, on my left.\nStep 3: I face away from the headboard by making a half turn.
